# pancake-strata: plan

A performance fork of [Strata](https://github.com/Niko1221/Strata) for single-user agent serving on one home box.
This file says what is true now, what runs next and what is waiting for a decision. Consolidated on 2026-11-15 from
the old 3,984-line plan and about 200 side documents, after an item-by-item audit against the code, presets, specs and
result archives.

Companion files:
- [TEST_LEDGER.md](TEST_LEDGER.md): every test ever run (ids `T-001` .. `T-967` at 2026-11-19, `T-nnn`), with numbers, spec, tier, verdict and
  the archive path; plus the data conflicts found while merging. Cite tests by id.
- [LEADS.md](LEADS.md): every unported idea from forks, upstream PRs, llama.cpp, papers and hardware notes (~600
  leads), with author numbers and whether our tree has it.
- `bench/specs/` (A/B specs), `bench/results/` and `pancake-docs/research/bench/results-*/` (raw evidence),
  `configs/pancake/` (presets), `tools/pancake/` (measurement suite).

## 1. Goal, box and models

Fastest single-user agent serving (long growing prompts, tool calls) with output identical to plain decoding unless a
flag says it is lossy. Speed = decode tok/s (new text and code edits), prompt tok/s at 4K/16K/32K, and time to the
first token of an agent turn after a long conversation.

| | |
|---|---|
| GPU | RX 7900 XT 20 GB, gfx1100, ROCm 10.0 (`/opt/rocm/core-10.0/lib`), hipBLASLt table `tools/hip/gfx1100-hipblaslt-100401.txt` |
| CPU / RAM | Ryzen 7 5700G (Zen 3, AVX2, no AVX-512, no WAITPKG), ~57 GB DDR4 (~33 GB/s; the CPU expert pool is bandwidth-bound) |
| Bus | PCIe 3.0 (forced by the 5700G), ReBAR 32 GB BAR0, IOMMU passthrough, THP madvise |
| Gone | RTX 3060 12 GB removed 2026-11-08: CUDA/sm_86-only items are parked (section 8); the owner's PC (GTX 1660 Ti 6 GB, sm_75) runs a small CUDA queue of its own (section 5 item 14) |
| Daily model | Qwen3.8-Flash-Next IQ2_XS (GSQ-RCO pack), preset `configs/pancake/flashnext-iq2_xs-fast-hip.json`, `run-pancake.sh` default |
| Opt-in | Flash-Next Q2_0 (`*-q2_0-*-adopted.json`; 134.7-136.1 tok/s, 80.6% top-1 vs Q8_0, T-377; the 15c Q2_0-vs-IQ2_XS redo is DROPPED: owner 2026-11-16, D8, keep IQ2_XS); Qwen3.6-35B-A3B (`src/qwen35` = strata-q36, and `strata-poly`); DeepSeek V4.1-Flash (`src/dsv41`, owner-deferred: Qwen first) |
| Not goals | Qwen3.8-27B dense, NVFP4 / `.basalt` (Blackwell), Vulkan/SYCL/Intel/Mac tuning, another engine as the base (vLLM, SGLang, KTransformers, TensorRT-LLM, LMDeploy, MLC-LLM, ExLlamaV2, PowerInfer, TGI, LightLLM, NanoFlow, DeepSpeed-MII, TensorFold: they keep the model in VRAM or need far more memory) |

Baseline (HEAD 693cdf63d, spec 43, T-392): short 111.5, edit 112.3, longdec10k 91.7 (noisy), longdec32k 102.8 (noisy)
tok/s. Reference points: stock Strata 54.5 vs ours 75.5 on the 3060 (T-216); Strata-rdna3 109-119 and Avalon 95-100
on this card (T-009); window budget short 24.8 ms = 15.9 GPU / 5.2 CPU / 2.2 other, edit 37.4 ms = 15.8 / 17.4 / 2.5 + 1.7 sync;
decode profile top kernels combine_cpu 25.8%, wait_flag 14.8%, rdna_kq 9.0%, expdec_gu 6.8% (spec 40 profile).

### Trend goal (proposed 2026-11-16, targets not claims; owner confirms in D12)

One number set, measured the same way every week: spec `bench/specs/115-trend-weekly.json` (the daily preset against
itself: the base arm is the week's trend point, the base-again arm is that week's noise floor), confirm settings, one
ledger row per run. A target counts as reached only by that run.

| measure | today (2026-11-16) | target 2026-12-14 | target 2027-02-08 | where it must come from |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| decode short, tok/s | 110.9-113.9 (T-612, T-614 bases, n=2) | 120 | 135 | R&D-A tier predictor, R&D-D GPU wait (section 6 items 19, 22) |
| decode edit, tok/s | 155.7 (T-612 base, n=2), 165.3 +-6.2 (T-588) | 175 | 200 | R&D-C window policy, R&D-A (items 21, 19) |
| prompt long1k / long3k / long64k, tok/s | 620 / 1106 / 1980 (T-614, n=1) | 680 / 1150 / 2000 | 750 / 1250 / 2200 | `DQ_FAST` if spec 103 confirms it, R&D-E (item 23) |
| warm agent turn, time to first token | measured 2026-11-17 (chain 9, T-810): a warm delta of 3,000 tokens costs 2.0-2.1 s of TTFT (about 0.46-0.50 ms a token plus a 0.6-0.8 s floor); the M5 sweep stopped early, cause in T-816 | chain 9 number (T-810) -15% | -30% | R&D-E warm-turn pipeline (item 23) |
| lossy lane (separate preset, never the daily base) | - | one gated arm >= +10% decode | a `fast-lossy` preset | R&D-B miss budget (item 20) |
| FIRST TREND POINT 2026-11-17 (T-822, spec 115 at confirm, base n=6) | decode short 106.9 +-7.6, edit 174.3 +-3.7; prompt long1k / long3k / long64k 801.2 / 1284.2 / 2405.7 tok/s | noise floor (base-again, n=4, same binary): short -11.0% +-21.4, edit +1.4% +-6.4, long1k -0.3% +-1.6, long3k +1.7% +-6.4, long64k -0.6% +-1.6 | - | the week's reference; PF_F16=1 (spec 161, T-820) would add +15..17% to the long prompt cases if adopted |

Why these sizes: the short window is 24.8 ms with 5.2 ms CPU and 2.2 ms other; halving the CPU part alone is +11.7%.
40.6% of the decode kernel time (combine_cpu 25.8% + wait_flag 14.8%) is the GPU waiting for CPU expert rows, so the
lever is fewer and cheaper misses and work inside the wait, not faster kernels: on 2026-11-16 the kernel-level and
launch-fusion switches screened as noise or losses (T-604, T-612, T-614, T-616), while the three large wins so far were
all policy: the ECAP row gate (edit +41.7%, T-588), the router lookahead (+10%, T-192) and the learned profile (+6.7%,
T-194). Trend rule: R&D time goes first to the track whose budget share is largest; a track is stopped after its
tier-0 (offline) step if the replay shows < 3% of window time to win.

## 2. Rules

Measurement (the full text is in AGENTS.md "Measurement rules"):
1. Paired A B B A runs, run 1 discarded, quiet machine (`abtest.py` waits and refuses beside a running server). Bar:
   >=2% end to end exact, >=3% lossy; TTFT bar >=0.1 s per warm turn or >=2% on long prompts. Screen, then confirm
   (`--block 3 --warmup 1 --outlier robust --reclaim-max 3 --reps 5`; owner 2026-11-16: 5 reps, not 9 (9 reps made the night round 10+ hours)). Owner rule since 2026-11-13: promote from 0% up
   at screen, decide at confirm; "test, never judge by reading".
2. Text identity is not an exactness proof (stock-vs-stock greedy noise is 1.2-1.3% of tokens; the base arm gave 6
   hashes in 6 runs). Lossy arms need token forcing (`quality_gate.py`, 20 requests x 300 tokens, 2 stock arms; it
   rejects only large deviations) and then the task gate (`task_gate.py`: LiveCodeBench subset + BFCL v4 multi-turn +
   own-repo grading) to choose between close variants.
3. Run `python tools/pancake/spec_lint.py` before any A/B: an arm key the fork does not read is a no-op.
4. Gains inside one substitute group do not add; price an idea against the window budget first (edits are a plain sum,
   so a small exact item caps at a few %). Re-measure fork claims here: Speedy's +22-33% on 3080s was noise..+9% here.
5. Every lead is ported and measured (owner, 2026-11-15; AGENTS.md rule 8): no lead is dropped by reasoning, only by a
   measurement. Three outcomes only: adopted (numbers in the commit), coded opt-in, not adopted (evidence + what would reopen it).
   Every test goes into TEST_LEDGER.md; nothing is dropped silently.

Code:
6. New features are opt-in; the default path stays byte-identical, and the commit says how that was checked. A default
   changes only after a paired measurement. Every new `STRATA_*` name is classified in
   `src/core/conversation_disk_env.def` (chunk-geometry switches are KeyIfSet in the disk key).
7. HIP is the benchmark platform; a CUDA-only change must not break HIP. Official model files + SHA-256 only;
   `presence_penalty=0`; no `STRATA_AUTO_PIN` for Qwen; `ROUTE_TAIL_SKIP=7` only by owner decision.
8. Credit every adopted idea in README section 9 in the same commit (fork, commit, author numbers).
   Screen reading (T-817, 2026-11-17): in 19 screens with an early and a late base run, 10 drifted > 5% (up to -55%); a screen
   PROMOTE under about +10%, or with a flagged `ORDER EFFECT`, is not evidence; the confirm tier decides.
9. Develop by day, run the GPU queue at night (`tools/pancake/night_queue.py`, `--dry-run` first).
10. Linux box repo on an ntfs3 disk (D): `git config checkout.workers 1`; no `reset --hard`, full checkouts or new
    worktrees there; merge only in the existing worktree.

  `power_dpm_force_performance_level` write, the ntfs3 mount); advice: drop the mount line and make os_tune.sh

  sudoers or permission settings themselves.

Test tiers (`abtest.py --tier`; answer each item at the cheapest tier that can):
- 0 offline: replays and byte counts (cache sims, `tools/pancake/trace/tail_replay.py`, `replay_turns.py --dry-run`); no GPU.
- 1 screen: one A B pass, short + edit, 2 requests per case per server; promote from 0% up (owner rule 2026-11-13,
  code 41f4f6231), drop if any case < -3%; decide at confirm (bar >= 2% exact, >= 3% lossy).
  Spec key `"prefill": true` keeps the `long*` cases (one cold request each, judged on prompt tok/s). ~2-3 min/arm.
  A screen promote is only a hint: n=2 gave intervals of +-100..+450 tok/s.
  `abtest.py --drift-check` (or `"drift_check": true` in the spec; coded 2026-11-16, in the 114a-f / 116 / 30 / 33 / 34 / 171-f22-wmma-q4-gfx11 / 108 / 109 / 112 specs): the base arm runs a second time
  at the end (arm `base-late`), every arm is judged against the mean of both base runs and the verdict block prints the base drift; above 5% it says ORDER EFFECT
  (a screen runs the base first: specs 83 and 93 had a base 11-40% low and every arm looked like a gain, T-606, T-622). Off by default.
- 2 confirm: A B B A with early stop (every arm/case decided or interval < 1.5% of base). For a decision use rule 1's
  flags with `--no-early-stop` and the same tier for every arm. Exact items need 12+ pairs to prove 2% at a paired SD of 1.5%.
- 3 rigorous (lossy, preset-default change, text differs from base, long-context claim): confirm + `quality_gate.py` +
  `task_gate.py SPEC.json --out bench/results/<day>-<name>` (`--dry-run` checks spec and task set without a server;
  base runs twice as the drift check; BETTER/PASS/INCONCLUSIVE/REJECT; INCONCLUSIVE -> `--reps 10-20` or more tasks).
- Evidence order: `ctest -L cpu`/`-L gpu` and parity -> kernel level (`expdec_bench`, `q2_rows_bench`, rocprof; kernel
  gain x window share >= 2% before end to end) -> screen -> confirm -> gates -> `python3 tools/pancake/promote.py <items.json>`.
- Cases: `long` 11.7K, `long32k`/`long64k` ~32000/~64000 tokens of `src/**/*.cpp`, 32 new tokens, each opened by a
  unique `[run <nonce>-<n>]` line (always cold, hash not compared); 128K is not an abtest case.
- Before a batch: `tools/pancake/os_tune.sh` (abtest warns below 20 GiB free in 2 MiB blocks; the arena locks 33 GiB;
  fix with `sudo tools/pancake/os_tune.sh compact|install`); CPU 3.9-4.1 GHz in run telemetry (slow runs sit at
  3.4-3.7 GHz; `sudo tools/pancake/os_tune.sh cpu-install`); engine on 8080 stopped; `pgrep -f test_phase.sh`.
  A server dying before ready is retried once, then the batch aborts with the free-block count.
- One spec per area with all its env-only arms. One decision per item: a "no difference" confirm -> opt-in or removed,
  retest only after a code change. A switch that never logged "acted" voids its arm. Arena vs mmap tiers differ ~8%,
  never mix them. Back to back: `tools/pancake/queue_run.sh OUTDIR SPEC[:abtest args] ...` (`-n` previews).
- No gain: at most 2-3 attempts. Read the `decode timing` line + `gpu_profile.py`; check the path acts (log line) and is
  not masked; tune with `tools/pancake/sweep.py`; then keep, opt-in or remove.

## 3. What the daily preset runs (fast-hip), with the evidence

| part | setting | evidence |
|---|---|---|
| RDNA ports from Strata-rdna3 | `GR_RDNA`, `GR_RDNA_Q8`, `GR_RDNA_A8`, `GR_INJ_UP`, `GR_FUSED`, `RDNA_Q6K`, `RDNA_MMVQ_WAVE=3`, `COMBINE_CPU`, `WAIT_FUSE`, `QFUSE`, `HEAD_MIX_MULTI` | decode 87-101 -> ~112 tok/s, T-009; round C best -> preset f0f9c5788, T-378 |
| expert tier | `--pcie-frac 0` (T-197; re-confirmed: 0.15/0.3/0.55 all slower, T-268), `--adapt-every 1 --adapt-swaps 32` (T-199), learned profile `data/expert-profile-pancake.bin` (+6.7%, T-194), GPU router lookahead default on (+10%, T-192), `ADAPT_PERSIST` default on |
| CPU pool | `POOL_FUSE_PHASES=1` (edit +2.5% +-1.7, T-224), `EXPDEC=1`, `EXPDEC_DOWN=1` (short +1.7% +-0.5, T-037/T-040) |
| drafting | `--spec 4` (official MTP depth; spec 2 -5%, spec 5/6 flat, T-118/T-149), `--spec-min-p 0.5`, `--lookup-max-t 12` (edit +8.8% +-6.8, quality PASS, T-126/T-127), English draft vocab |
| prompt path | hipBLASLt 100401 table (1,345 -> 1,850-1,920 tok/s, T-096), `PF_GEMM`, `PF_F16=1` (ADOPTED 2026-11-17 by the owner, was 2 = the F16 kernels off: confirm T-820 +15.1 / +16.7 / +17.3% prompt tok/s at 14K / 32K / 64K, no effect at 3K or on decode, quality gate T-828 rate and event rules PASS, near-tie column 98.3% FAIL: information), `PF_FUSED=1` (see section 7: no HIP A/B recorded), `PF_PAD`, `PREFILL_AUTO_MAX=32768`, `PREFILL_CPU_SHARE=0.6`/`_MAX=4096`, `PREFILL_RING=192`, `SELECT_WMMA`, `HIP_WMMA=1`, Speedy opt-ins `PF_GROUP_SCRATCH`, `PF_HC_REGION`, `PROMPT_ATTN_IMMA`, `KV_PREFETCH` (taken from Speedy's numbers, no HIP A/B recorded) |
| attention / KV | `--kv int8 --kv-resident 32768 --max-context 131072`, `ATTN_DAILY`, `QSA_ROWS`, `PA_FAST`, `KV_HOST_DMA` |
| ECAP and cache (adopted 2026-11-15, owner; ECAP changed to the gated arm 2026-11-16) | `STRATA_VERIFY_ECAP=0` with `STRATA_VERIFY_ECAP_MIN_ROWS=9` (cap 0 only in windows of 9 or more rows: edit 165.3 +-6.2 tok/s = +41.7% +-5.6 over no ECAP, short +2.8% +-2.6, T-588; token forcing PASS -0.011 pts, T-589; tool-call gate PASS, T-591; before: cap 1, edit +33.5% +-14.3, T-270, T-310, base edit prompt 147.6 tok/s, T-560), `--conversation-cache-mib 2048 --conversation-cache-min-tokens 8192` (prompt time -21.6%, T-183); sampling default temperature 0.1, top_p 0.95, top_k 20 |
| deferral | `VERIFY_DEFER_CPU` OFF (owner decision 17.1; contested, section 4) |
| server | grammar on (spec decoding mask 0.1-0.2% of a window, T-315), thinking caps `reasoning_budget_grace: 512` + close retry, `env_to_user`, `effort_position: end`, loop guard, tolerant tool calls, turn log, `AGENT_COUNTERS=1` (log only, T-513) |

Lossy switches in fast-hip (an exact bundle = fast minus these 11): `GDN_CONVL2`, `GR_RDNA_A8`, `GR_RDNA_Q8`, `HC_UPMIX`,
`PA_FAST`, `PF_F16=1` (was 2 until 2026-11-17, D5), `PF_HCDOWN`, `PREFILL_AUTO_MAX=32768`, `PREFILL_CPU_SHARE=0.6`, `ROUTE_TAIL_SKIP=0`,
`SELECT_WMMA`. So the daily base is not bit-exact; "exact arm" in a spec means exact relative to this base.

Retired presets (2026-11-15, moved to `configs/pancake/retired/`, restore with `git mv`; `run-pancake.sh turbo` exits with a
message): `flashnext-iq2_xs-turbo-hip.json` / `-turbo.json` = fast + `ROUTE_PRIOR=0.5` (+ `VERIFY_DEFER_CPU=1`, minus
`AGENT_COUNTERS` on HIP) (lossy; edit +29.7% n.s., token forcing INCONCLUSIVE +0.83 pts, T-245; all-prose -8%, T-387;
interaction with ECAP untested) and `flashnext-iq2_xs-hip.json` (exact copy with no users). The one daily preset is
`flashnext-iq2_xs-fast-hip.json`; Q2_0 `*-adopted.json` carry FUSE_PHASES, EXPDEC_DOWN,
lookup 12, no deferral. Not in any preset: `ROUTE_PRIOR_AUTO`, `VERIFY_DEFER_AUTO` (-12.5% IQ2_XS, T-262),
`KV_RING_ADAPT`, `PARK_REWIND_MAX`, `PARK_DIVERGENCE_LOG`, `PREFILL_EQUAL_TAIL`, `ADAPT_KMISS`, `PARK_TRUNCATE`,
`SUFFIX_INDEX`, `VERIFY_ECAP_MIN_ROWS`, the matrix-core kernels (`STRATA_RDNA_WMMA_MINCOLS`).

Served instance: `tools/pancake/pancake.service` (systemd user unit, `run-pancake.sh iq2_xs fast` via `NOCAP=1`, port
11437, 127.0.0.1 only, `MemoryMax=54G`, `Conflicts=` the old units; not enabled at boot). Start `systemctl --user start
pancake` (ready in 1-3 min, log says `ready:`), stop it before any benchmark (it holds the whole card). Portal
`http://127.0.0.1:11437/` and `/pancake`; opencode provider `strata` points at it. Live test: T-556. The old upstream
`strata.service` and its config are in `retired-2026-11-15` folders on the box.

Interactions: `ROUTE_PRIOR` set (even 0) disables ROUTE_PUBLISH / ROUTE_FAST / ROUTER_FUSE; deferral turns off 6
fused paths process-wide (so A35 / ROUTER_FUSE "no-op under deferral" no longer holds with deferral off);
`ADAPT_KMISS` needs `--adapt-async 1`; `*_AUTO` and the fixed switch never together; `KV_RING_ADAPT` under `--serve`
follows `--max-context`; CPU share binds only for deltas <= ~400 tokens; PARK_REWIND helps only when admission is the
miss.

Deploy (the owner copies the script): `scp tools/pancake/box_chains/bench-updatedRD.sh dan@192.168.100.82:/home/dan/clone-of-d/src/` then, on the box, `cd /home/dan/clone-of-d/src && nohup sh ./bench-updatedRD.sh > /dev/null 2>&1 &` (it waits for `WINCHAINAA_DONE` and `WINBENCHUS_DONE` itself, and fetches the three source trees from GitHub); the log is `bench-updatedRD.log`, results `/home/dan/auto-runs/benchRD-*`.

**BENCHMARKS FIRST, SHORT (owner order 2026-11-19, autonomy: "prioritize those benchmarks on the box - make sure they are not taking multiple hours - dont forget to run the script for tuneup"; T-976):** the two benchmarks (updated-strata against plain upstream v0.2.16, updated-strata-rdna3 against plain Strata-rdna3) no longer wait for the end of chain AA (about 12 h). New scheduler `tools/pancake/box_chains/bench-sched.sh` (box log `/home/dan/clone-of-d/src/bench-sched.log`, last line `BENCHSCHED_DONE`): (a) sends SIGSTOP to exactly one pid, the AA shell whose cmdline is exactly `sh ./redo-chainAA.sh` (AA is a sequential `sh`: it freezes between steps, the step that runs now finishes, nothing is killed); (b) waits for 3 idle minutes (the frozen AA shell is invisible to busy(); the chains test `aa_live`: an AA shell that is not in state T still counts as a running job); (c) runs `bench-updatedAA.sh` then `bench-updatedRD.sh` with `BENCH_SCHED=1` (no wait for AA), `BENCH_PREIDLED=1` (only the 30 s quick idle), `BENCH_KEEP_SERVICE_DOWN=1` (pancake.service stays down, AA needs the box without the service); a chain that aborts writes its marker, the next one runs anyway; (d) SIGCONT to the AA shell on every exit (normal end, error, HUP / INT / TERM), after checking that the pid still is `sh ./redo-chainAA.sh`. Only SIGKILL of the scheduler leaves AA frozen: `kill -CONT $(pgrep -fx 'sh ./redo-chainAA.sh')`. The chains were cut with the two-pass protocol and a time budget (`BENCH_BUDGET_S` 7200 s; optional passes are skipped when it is over): screen first (1 start per arm and the base again), confirm only for arms with a screen benefit (>= 1.0 % lossless / >= 1.5 % lossy, `chainT-decide.py`), 3 reps instead of 5 and only the decode cases (paired SD short 3.2 %, edit 2.7 %); the long set 10000 / 32000 / 64000 / 100000 tokens is ONE screen rep per arm without the drift arm (paired SD of a long prompt 1.3 %); builds one after the other, ninja -j8, outside `/`; one 3-minute idle at the start and 30 s before each part. ESTIMATE per chain (T-949 calibration, +-25 %): upstream chain 2.0 h (sync 5 min, builds 15, tune-up 29, spec 220 15 + 25, spec 221 35), RDNA3 chain 1.9 h (sync 5, builds 15, ctests 3, tune-up 25, spec 250 30 + 25, spec 251 15), about 3.9 h together (before: 5.5-8 h each); a full strata build on this box took 3.6 min on 2026-11-19, not the 1.5-2.5 h the old header assumed. TUNE-UP in both chains (read only, no sudo, nothing applied): hardware JSON, `pcie_link.py`, `host_tune.sh --check`, `host_tune_ab.sh plan`, `os_tune.sh` (its check mode; `os_tune.sh install` is the owner's), and the flag diagnostics (upstream chain: plain build STRATA_HIPBLASLT_TUNING + STRATA_PF_F16 10 min, updated build STRATA_RDNA_Q6K, STRATA_GR_RDNA, STRATA_RDNA_MMVQ_WAVE, STRATA_COMBINE_CPU, STRATA_VERIFY_ECAP 18 min, the table is not repeated on the updated build; RDNA3 chain: POOL_FUSE_PHASES + ctest parity, VERIFY_ECAP + ctest, PF_F16, RDNA_MMVQ_WAVE, KV_HOST_DMA, 20 min; its plain-build diagnostics and HIPBLASLT_TUNING / EXPERT_PROFILE are dropped because spec 250 and spec 251 measure exactly those); every exact flag gets the parity smoke and the 2-prompt screen of `flag_diag`; verdicts in `/home/dan/auto-runs/winbenchus/` (`diag-plain/`, `diag-updated/`, `tuneup/`, `tuned-flags-recommended.json`) and `/home/dan/auto-runs/benchRD-diag-updated/`, `benchRD-tuneup/`, `benchRD-recommended/`; only exact or gate-passing GAIN flags enter the regenerated recommended-flags file. Found on the way: `tools/pancake/flag_diag.py` is not on port/rdna3-decode, so the old upstream chain would have stopped at its SYNC check; the chain now runs flag_diag from the downloaded updated-strata tree.

**FIRST SCHEDULED RUN FAILED, FIXED, RE-SCHEDULED (2026-11-19 13:26-13:38, T-977):** both chains failed fast and measured nothing. Upstream chain: the plain-upstream config lacked `--spec` / `--prefill` (needed by a native pack), `rocminfo` was not on PATH (no diagnostics verdicts); both builds compiled, including the HIP code of updated-strata. RDNA3 chain: the plain Strata-rdna3 build needs `rccl/rccl.h`, which this ROCm lacks (the include is unused). Fixed: documented upstream flags in the plain config, ROCm bin on PATH, PREREQ check, a PREBUILD SMOKE after each build (engine start + one short request, at most 15 min: a broken build or launch ends the chain with the engine's error line), an empty rccl stub header on CPLUS_INCLUDE_PATH for the RD builds, error lines of failed screens in the chain log. Re-scheduled at the same step boundary (`bench-sched.v3.sh`, AA frozen again). New estimate: upstream chain 2.1 h (smokes +6 min), RDNA3 chain 2.0 h; abort within about 15 minutes of the first build if a build or launch is broken.

**SECOND RD ATTEMPT FAILED, FIXED (2026-11-19 14:34-14:37, T-978):** the plain Strata-rdna3 build failed again: the empty rccl stub was wrong, because `src/core/peer_transport.inc` (experimental rccl peer transport) declares RCCL types and function pointers. Their `--mtp-q4 all` rewrites the draft head in place as Q4_0, which needs a head stored in >= 4.5 bits (their IQ3_S head is Q6_K); the box's IQ2_XS head is IQ4_XS (1360 < 1440 bytes per row). Model-file dependency, so bench_updatedRD_conf.py substitutes `--mtp-q4 proj` only for such a head (read from the GGUF header, CONFIG SUBST line); every other flag of theirs stays. Queue on the box: run-queue-rd3.sh (RD v8, exact RD part, RD task gate v2), stops after step 1 if the RD run aborts.

**RETEST OF WHAT WAS DROPPED, RULES FIXED, ONE-BY-ONE CONFIRM QUEUE (2026-11-20; T-1003):** the review found 9 flags / arms dropped or changed without a valid retest (RD 250 arms dropped at screen despite edit +43 %; POOL_FUSE_PHASES removed on 1 rep + Not-Run ctests; --lookup-max-t removed on a whole-arm gate; US 222/223 exact never confirmed / finished; RD 251 arms called no difference at +-23..233 %; the pass-2 cap left 8 flags RETEST; slices 1-2 DROP on drifted data; ECAP and 3 kernels removed on short-only / text evidence (restored); PF_F16=1 shipped on 1 rep). Owner rules now in the tools: (1) TWO-PASS: any case >= 1.0 % exact / 1.5 % lossy earns pass 2, a case below -3 % does not block it; DROP only from a clean pass 2 (drift <= 5 %), drift > 5 % = INCONCLUSIVE; pass 1 without a benefit = NO-GAIN-P1 (no change); no pass-2 cap (the budget's rest is carried). (2) CONFIRM ONE BY ONE: every confirm is one flag in its own paired block (base, flag x 3 interleaved, base; ~17.5 min). (3) FLAGS JUDGED INDIVIDUALLY: a bundle / whole-arm result never removes a flag, it only triggers per-flag arms. (4) OPTIONAL: a clean confirm with a case >= +10 % and a case <= -3 % = opt-in (not default; shipped file 'optional' section + README-PANCAKE.md, with the cases it helps / hurts). PENDING RETEST: POOL_FUSE_PHASES stays out of the RD shipped file, --lookup-max-t out of the US shipped file, PF_F16=1 stays in the RD file, until their own paired blocks decide. QUEUE (box run-queue-sweep3.sh, harness pinned, each slice <= 2.5 h under bench-sched.v8.sh, AA frozen, service down): retest rdC-1, rdC-2, mixC-3, usC-4, mixR-5, mixR-6, usR-7 (RD priority list, US priority list incl. the 7 core flags, then the RD 250 bundle reference, POOL_FUSE_PHASES, PF_F16, the 251 arms, US 222 bundle, the --lookup-max-t gate, 223) ~16 h; then rdB-1..4, usB-1..9, rdR-1..2 (blocked pass 1 + one-by-one pass 2) <= 37.5 h; then carry-rd, carry-us; log run-queue-sweep3.log, last line QUEUE_SWEEP3_DONE. Then apply_triage.py on <branch>-triage.v2.json, per flag (separate commit).

ETA: slice 1 ~12:45, preliminary ~14:45, screens end ~21:20, stage 2 + final shift by the same ~2 h (final ~09:30 next day). Stage-2b ranking: the highest low-gain flags go first. **PROTOCOL CLARIFIED 2026-11-20 ~11:05 (T-1005, run-queue-us5.sh):** screen all flags once, RESCREEN (second 1-run test) the ones above 0 % (high gain >= 2 % first, low gain after the final comparison), the verdict comes from the two runs together (KEEP when the benefit repeats, OPTIONAL when a >= +10 % case and a <= -3 % case both repeat, DROP when it does not); no 3-rep confirm stage, no preliminary stock comparison; gates only for --lookup-max-t and lossy flags >= 3 %; the FINAL 5-rep comparison validates the set. ETAs: final ~03:15 next day, low-gain rescreens ~11:00. **TRIAGE CORRECTED ~11:10 (T-1005, run-queue-us6.sh):** run 1 is classified per case: ANY case >= +2 % = HIGH (rescreened, may end OPTIONAL), best case 0-2 % = LOW (backlog file us-backlog-low.json, next week with the 31 deferred switches), all cases <= 0 % = NO-GAIN (plus arm, dropped) or HURTS (preset flag, rescreened once, no removal from one run); the rescreen is only for HIGH + HURTS flags; the two-run rule decides. ETAs: final ~03:45 next day; the low-gain rescreens are gone.

**DRIFT OF THE US SCREEN FIXED IN THE JUDGEMENT (2026-11-20; T-1006):** every block of slice usS1-1of5 drifted (a step between a fast and a slow box state, 10-15 min windows, prefill -30 %, edit -12 %; not heat or clock; cause not proven), 0 of 20 arms had a verdict. From the next slice (run-queue-us7.sh, bench-sweep.v6.sh): blocks of 4 arms, a base start after every 2, each arm judged against the two bases around it (5 % guard on that pair, 8 % when both show the same prefill speed), no whole-block re-run, the unclean arms run again alone. Simulated on the measured state process: 45 % of the arms judged (was 12 %), 3.9 starts per judged arm (was 19.6). Slice 1 itself is unchanged and will mostly stay INCONCLUSIVE; see TEST_LEDGER.md T-1006.

   **QUEUE PROTOCOL: TWO PASSES, DUMP AFTER PASS 1 (owner order 2026-11-18 evening, TEST_LEDGER.md T-949; applies to every queued and future test).** Pass 1 = one cheap run of every arm (abtest screen, replay 1 rep, gates). Pass 2 (confirm, extra reps, replay reps 2-3) runs ONLY for arms whose pass-1 point estimate shows a benefit: faster by >= 1% end to end for lossless arms, >= 1.5% for lossy arms (half the standing bars), and for replays prompt time <= -1% against base; flat and worse arms are DROPPED and recorded in the ledger as `dropped after pass 1: no benefit observed (n=1 pass, not a refutation)` with their numbers, no further runs. Promotion bars are unchanged (>= 2% e2e, >= 3% lossy, drift check, quality gate, 5-rep confirm). The thresholds live in one place, the `PASS2_*` constants of `tools/pancake/box_chains/chainT-decide.py` (`--replay` filters replay arms), tested in `tools/pancake/test_box_chains.py`. Deployed 2026-11-18 20:02 box clock: chain T relaunched as the new script (it was still an idle waiter), chain X relaunched, U and V have no second pass, S is finished. Never reduce pass-1 reps below what the standing bar needs for a decisive confirm (T-938 / T-940). **REMAINING HOURS (measured calibration, T-949; T started 20:02:35 box clock; best / expected / worst = 0 / 25 / 50% of arms show a benefit):** chain T 4.9 / 10.6 / 19.3 h, U 0, V 1.0 / 1.7 / 4.0 h, tail X 6.9 / 10.6 / 18.1 h; main path T + U + V 5.9 / 12.3 / 23.3 h = finish about 02:00 / 08:20 / 19:20 on 2026-11-19; with X 12.8 / 22.9 / 41.4 h = 08:50 on 11-19 / 18:55 on 11-19 / 13:25 on 11-20. 13. **Host tuning, independent tests per step (RAN in chain H T-848 and H2 T-942: steps NOT measured in H, H2 left the host changed, see the owner-decision note above; queued 2026-11-16; T-674 offline PASS, T-675..T-677 NOT MEASURED).** Settings come from reading bramvanoploo/strata-performance-setup (MIT, README.md section 9); one script does both jobs: `tools/pancake/host_tune.sh` (`--list`, `--check` read-only, `--apply NAME[,NAME]` runtime only, `--revert NAME|all`, `--status`, `--probe`, `--mlock-check`, `--dry-run`, `--persist` for the boot-time files; every apply saves the previous value to a state file and revert restores exactly it; writes go through `sudo tee`, no sudoers or permission edits) is the test harness and the way for other people to tune their host (README.md section 8, Host tuning). Steps: `nvme_ra` (read_ahead_kb 128 -> 4096, scheduler none), `thp` (enabled always, defrag defer+madvise), `swappiness` (60 -> 10), `memlock` (limits.d, no runtime form, tested by the mlock check), `cpu_perf` (governor + EPP performance), `amd_pstate` (active), `gpu_dpm` (high, discrete cards only), `aspm` (performance), `cstate` (states above 10 us off); report only: `iommu` (iommu=pt, multi-GPU), `headless` (never applied: the owner's box uses remote desktop). Box 2026-11-16: governor/EPP already performance and card1 already high, so `cpu_perf` and `gpu_dpm` are skipped there by the probe (no A/B possible). A/B: `tools/pancake/host_tune_ab.sh plan` then `run` (stop pancake.service only after 3 idle minutes without a client on 11437, restart at the end): baseline, each step (apply, `bench/specs/125-host-tune-ab.json` at `--tier screen`, revert), then all steps together, then a baseline again for drift; results `/home/dan/auto-runs/winH-<step>.stdout` and `.jsonl`, table with `host_tune_ab.sh table`; judge a step by its base arm against the baseline and above the base-again noise of the same file; estimate 3.5 to 4 hours (T-675). Cold pack load for `nvme_ra` (three starts after `drop_caches`, `load_time.py`, T-676). mlock: `host_tune_ab.sh mlock` after the engine start (VmLck against the 33 GiB arena, T-677; fix on a FAIL: `host_tune.sh --apply memlock --persist`, new login, restart; the pancake.service unit has no LimitMEMLOCK). `STRATA_PF_AHEAD` already has specs (section 5 item 2, spec 103; section 6 item 34, spec 126): no new one. The shortlist head and per-layer-share placement tests are section 6 item 33 (other agents), not here.

One queue runner does them one at a time: `tools/pancake/win_cuda_queue.ps1` (PowerShell 5.1; order and needs in `tools/pancake/win_cuda_queue_plan.json`; results in `bench/results/<date>-gtx1660ti-queue/<item>/`; self test `tools/pancake/test_win_cuda_queue.py`). Order and estimate (not measured): `00-build` 75 min, `01-poly-cpu-tests` 3, `02-poly-gpu-synthetic` 10, `03-head-parity` 5, `04-compile-fixed-tests` 6, `05-poly-expert-parity` 20, `06-poly-cpu-experts-bench` 15, `07-poly-draft-race` 25, `08` spec 159 80, `09` spec 160 35; with the 3-minute idle waits about 5.1 h.

   - **B, runs with a small change:** (1) the A/B arms of `strata-poly`: `bench_win.py` takes ONE arm (extra `strata-poly` args on its command line, env from the process), so the new `tools/pancake/bench_win_ab.py` reads a spec and calls it once per arm, base first and base-again last: specs 159 (`--threads 4`, `--cpu-multi`, `--attn-split`, `--graph-experts`, `--kv-q8`, `--expert-swaps 0`, `--vram-reserve-mib 444`) and 160 (`STRATA_SOFTMAX_REG=0`, `STRATA_GEMM_SMALL_TILED=0`); decode only, `--max-context 2048` is fixed by `bench_win.py`, screen tier, spec_lint clean. (2) Four tests did not compile with MSVC in T-552: `poly_kv_q4_test` (range-for over a braced list of pairs, `src/poly/kv_q4_test.cu:342`, needs a named array of pairs), `q5k_head_test` (`std::to_string` without `<string>`, `src/kernels/cuda/q5k_head_test.cu:95`, add the include), `flag_order_test` (`__atomic_load_n`, `tests/core/flag_order_test.cu:31`, use `std::atomic` or `_InterlockedOr`) and `gemm_w16_parity` (`setenv`, `src/prefill/gemm_w16_parity.cu:101`, use `_putenv_s`); the last two also need `-DSTRATA_BUILD_TESTS=ON`, which the build step does not set (the sm_75 build tree has it OFF). Item `04` runs the first two after the fix and skips them while the exe is missing.
   - **C, cannot run here:** `strata` (Flash-Next) locks a ~33 GiB expert arena (README.md:882; 13.9 GB RAM here); `strata-q36` needs the `qwen36-ud-q4_k_xl` pack (`configs/pancake/qwen36-ud-q4_k_xl-q36.json:12`, not on this PC; only the IQ2_XXS gguf is) and shares the arena design; `STRATA_FATTN` / `STRATA_FDEC` need Ampere (`src/poly/fattn.cu:40` and `:624` `ampere_mma_available`, `:273-281` `mma16816` is `__trap()` below sm_80, `src/poly/fattn_mma.cuh:1788` Turing refuses more than 32 columns, `src/poly/qwen35.cpp:193` and `:205` log "needs an Ampere or later NVIDIA card"; `poly_fattn_test` is the same); the `strata` prompt kernels compiled out below sm_80 (cp.async, bf16 mma, tf32: `src/prefill/kernels.cu:2807`, `src/prefill/moe_fused.cu:182`, `src/prefill/moe_fused_iq.cu:163`, `src/kernels/cuda/qsa_prompt_attn.cu:46`, `qsa_select.cu:371`, `native_qsa_score.cu:39`; the sm_75 fallbacks of `include/strata/core/tc_sm75.hpp` and `STRATA_SM75_PERF` live in `strata` / `strata-q36`, which cannot load here); `abtest.py` and `replay_turns.py` (`/proc`, `os.sysconf`: `tools/pancake/abtest.py:109,183,563`); HIP and two-GPU items (this PC has one NVIDIA card).
   - **Guards of the runner:** starts only 23:00-06:30 local (`-WindowStart`, `-WindowEnd`); `-Now` ignores the window and keeps every other guard; before each item it needs 3 idle minutes in a row (no GPU use above 15%, CPU below 20%, no engine, compiler or other benchmark process, free RAM and VRAM above the item's need, AC power, and in night mode no keyboard or mouse for 10 minutes); stop file `C:\Users\iancu\build-latest\STOP` is read between items and steps; one copy only (named mutex); no process is ever killed by name, only the one process the runner started itself when its step times out; no power or system setting is touched. `-Plan` prints the order and the needs and runs nothing. Start by hand: `powershell -NoProfile -File tools\pancake\win_cuda_queue.ps1 -Plan`, then `... -Now` (or let the night window apply). **"Scheduled" means: the plan, the runner and the 23:00 window are written; no Windows scheduled task exists yet.** The main session registers one after the owner confirms the points in this item's report: the shared build tree `build-sm75` (the build step reuses it, so it must not run beside another build), the `-Now` start while the owner is at the PC, the timeouts, and that the queue stops its own timed-out process.

Rules taken from their planner: decline instead of guessing, a lever must win by a margin (10%), memory is a ledger with a kill-class margin (20% under the 54G cap) and is never raised for a speed model, lossy knobs are marked and `--no-lossy` drops them all (the daily preset carries them, so the default run keeps them), the tier floor is the model's token cycle. What is derived and what is not: DERIVED 7 (VRAM tier = free VRAM less dense, KV, reserve, draft head, late allocation; `--pcie-frac` 0; the prompt chunk 32768 from the 90% loan rule; the context 131072 from the RAM ledger; workers = cores - 1), ANCHORED 1 (`--adapt-swaps` 32: scaled from the preset by the promotion break-even, reproduces 32 by construction, a hypothesis anywhere else), CARRIED 10 (KV, drafting, conversation cache, CPU share: no rule over facts decides them, the ledger does). The multi-client shape that does apply: clients alternating over more conversations than `--conversation-cache-slots` / the 2048 MiB budget make every request a switch = a park + a restore of 1-1.7 GiB (inferred). MEASURED, T-563 on the nine spec-49 runs: 219 parks mean 164 ms max 771 ms, 60 restores mean 81 ms; T-172: 55,424 tokens (1,258 MiB) parked in 706 ms = 1.74 GiB/s; T-168: HIP park 110 ms under 4K tokens, 212 ms at 25K, 0.3-1.4 s at 50-77K. MEASURED counts, spec 49 stdout: the daily preset arm (`cache2048-min8192`) parks 12 / 8 / 9 times and restores 7 in a 75-request session (41 parks refused by the 8192 minimum), the old base 53 / 51 / 54; the preset's prompt time is 63.1 s lower (-21.6% +-14.8, T-183) because 3 cold re-reads of about 80 s each become 0. INFERRED upper bound of the park time in those runs (snapshot MiB / 1.74 GiB/s, `tools/pancake/state_cost.py --replay-stdout`, T-689): preset 7.5-11.2 s = 3.3-5.0% of prompt time and 1.7-2.2% of turn time (an upper bound: the snapshot counts the kept K/V prefix and the RAM-side checkpoint copies that never cross PCIe). The turn log's `other` (duration - prompt - decode) is 4% for agent turns but does NOT bound these: parks and checkpoints sit inside prompt_ms. THE REAL TRADE-OFF: the return is workload-dependent: -21.6% prompt time on the 11-09 replay (a 34K and a 70K conversation interleaved with probes), but only 1.89% of request time over 2,036 opencode requests (T-437, simulated; 12.2% of the time is cold re-reads with no cache at all). (a) I2 `STRATA_LOOKAHEAD_CTRL=1` (measurement, implies `STRATA_LOOKAHEAD_STATS`, `include/strata/program/lookahead_ctrl.hpp`, `RouterLookahead::enable_ctrl`, expert_source.cpp `stats_score`): adds to the existing stats print, every 100 windows, one summary line and one line per layer `strata lookahead ctrl[ layer L]: windows= slots= stale_gpu= stale_cpu= control= prev=`. All figures are the share of the TRUE routed slots (all experts, resident or not, per token and layer) found in the predicted set of the SAME size k as the true routing, which is the BigMoeOnEdge denominator (ours in STATS is the non-resident misses only, hence 19-29% vs their 80-89%, MOD_PLAN.md item 29). stale_gpu = what the engine acts on (the GPU table by default); stale_cpu = the host BF16 router of layer l+1 on layer l's input, computed in addition (copies the rows in `submit` when CTRL is on), so stale_gpu vs stale_cpu checks the GPU look-ahead; CONTROL = layer l's own router on its own true input, read back against the routing it just made, must read 1.0000 (ties go to the lower id; NOT with `ROUTE_PRIOR` / `BUDGET` / `ROUTE_RESIDENT` on: those change the committed top-k on purpose; below 1 otherwise the probe or a router copy is wrong and the 19-29% is a bug, not a property); prev = the previous token of the window as predictor (the I1 base rate). Costs a router GEMV per layer on the dispatch thread and one on the predictor thread: a measurement run only, and the predictor busy ms rises. Parser: `python tools/pancake/lookahead_ctrl.py engine.log` (per-layer table + verdict; last block wins; pytest 4). TO RUN (box agent): the daily preset + `STRATA_LOOKAHEAD_CTRL=1` for one served short and one edit request of at least 100 windows each, read the verdict line; it needs a look-ahead thread, so on the arena preset also `STRATA_POOL_PREREAD=1` (or `STRATA_LOOKAHEAD_STATS` is what starts the thread: CTRL implies it, so nothing else is needed). (b) I6 `STRATA_LOOKAHEAD_SKIP_DROPPED=1` (`lookahead_skip.hpp`): with `STRATA_MISS_SKIP_W` / `_CUM` set, predicted experts the skip rule would drop (share = softmax of the host router logits of the token's top-k picks; the engine's own rule `share < W` / `mass above >= CUM`; an expert is left cold only when every token that predicts it would drop it) are not warmed and not handed to the Foresight swap; without the rule it prints a note and does nothing. PREMISE CHECK: in OUR engine the skip rule reads GPU residency, so a warm of file or arena pages does not un-drop an expert (their mechanism); only a Foresight swap copy (`STRATA_FS_SLOTS`) does. What it saves is wasted warms: expected small. Spec 135. (c) I4 `STRATA_ROUTE_RANGE=L` (LOSSY; `route_range.hpp`, mode of `route_prior_k` in `src/kernels/cuda/route_prior.cu`, not compiled here): the route-prior bonus is L x the TOKEN'S own logit range (max - min of the 512 logits) instead of lambda; `STRATA_ROUTE_PRIOR` only has to be set to a value above 0, L replaces it, and it wins over `STRATA_ROUTE_PRIOR_DELTA` (that one scales by the LAYER'S running mean range; this one is BigMoeOnEdge's per-token rule, which is scale- and shift-invariant, tested). Rule: `verify_ecap_drop_rows` (the scalar `verify_ecap_drop` is now a call with no list and no gate); unset, the result equals the old rule: `src/core/verify_ecap_test.cpp` compares it with a frozen copy of the old rule on 3,000 random windows plus a one-value list and a never-firing gate (T-723); three mutants of the new code (gate sign, row index, cap compare) are killed. Coded: `STRATA_ADAPT_VICTIM_UNUSED=1` (+ `STRATA_ADAPT_VICTIM_K`, default 4; `STRATA_ADAPT_VICTIM_FALLBACK=rule|skip`): victims with no use in the last K rounds first, the used ones only as fallback (rule) or never (skip); `STRATA_ADAPT_DIRECT_SWAP=1`: no readback of the victim's host pages. What already existed (read from generate.cpp adapt()): one host-to-device copy per swap straight over the victim's slot; the victim (lowest decayed count) is known before the window, the INCOMING expert only at the round (best non-resident candidate by decayed count, floor 2.0); `STRATA_ADAPT_OVERLAP` lands the copies while the next window runs, `STRATA_ADAPT_IDLE_COPY` (T-613 crash, no speed number), `STRATA_ADAPT_COPY2`, `STRATA_ADAPT_LAYERWAIT`; the ledger has no measured per-expert copy time or adaptive copy wait for the daily preset (T-016: window 24.8 ms short / 37.4 ms edit, 28.5 swaps a window on the edit block; T-021 old blocking swap ~5.6 ms a round; the only adapt-wait number, 0.41 ms with `--adapt-async 1`, is a community RTX 3090 run). Coded: `STRATA_ADAPT_SPEC_COPY=1` (+ `_SLOTS` 4 per layer, `_MAX` 8 copies a round, `_CONF` gain >= 1.0, `_AGE` 4 rounds): at the end of a round the tier's own rule runs on predicted counts (decay x counts + this window's increment) and the predicted incoming experts are copied into a bounded VRAM staging area on a second stream (a slot is rewritten only after the round's last read, read only after the staging stream's event); a swap of a staged expert is a device copy from the staging slot, every other swap the normal host copy. Replay (T-956, CIs over windows): 11-13% of the incoming experts staged, 61-62% of the staged copies unused, 1.2x PCIe bytes, at most 0.30 ms of a 24.8 ms window (1.2%; all copy time counted as exposed, so an upper bound), below the screen noise; 4 slots / 32 copies: 35% staged, 1.6x bytes, at most 1.0 ms (4%). The CPU cost is 220-270 us a round (T-955), the same order as the saving at the default size. Local GPU test on a GTX 1660 Ti, NOT gfx1100 (T-957 / T-958): pinned expert copies 204 / 216 / 252 us by layer format (uniform within a layer format, CV 2-4%, not across formats: the bytes differ by 28%), the staged scheme lands a round 5.4% [5.1,5.6] sooner at 11% staged, 40% sooner at a hypothetical 58% staged, every slot byte-identical to the baseline. Specs 216 (screen) / 217 (confirm); chain Z (T-959, after chain Y). Expected: no gain with this predictor; the lever is the predictor (the next window's routing), not the copy. |

The preset sets `STRATA_PF_HC_REGION=1`; `Prefill::carve` then takes xn16 / lo / lo16 / gated from the start of the region the MoE set also starts at (logits, w, ids, slot_dev ...), and `moe_combine_write_norm_rs` writes xn16 in block t while later blocks still read slot_dev / w / Dm (the two-kernel path is ordered by the kernel boundary, which is the invariant the `pf_hc_region` comment states). If the no-hc-region arm still faults, the cause is not this alias.

  generate.cpp ~14982) from `idle_pace` while the window's kernels run, where the default path issues them all at the round's
  end; the queue itself is in layer order (swaps stable-sorted by layer, generate.cpp 14846). Diagnosis queued as chain 13
  (`/home/dan/clone-of-d/src/win-chain13.sh`, after `WINCHAIN12_DONE`, results `/home/dan/auto-runs/win13-*`): spec 114a
  (`STRATA_ADAPT_SIGNAL=host`: the host-function signal instead), 114b (`STRATA_ADAPT_IDLE_COPY_KB=1048576`: every copy at the
  round's end through the same queue), 114c (`STRATA_VERIFY_TRACE=1`: where the stream stopped). The fix follows from which arm runs.
  CODE 2026-11-16 (T-713, host-tested, engine not built; breadcrumb only, the switch does the same as before): `STRATA_ADAPT_IDLE_COPY_TRACE=1` (default off, Inert) makes `Verifier::adapt_gate_wait` print `strata verify: adapt idle-copy trace: <wait|landed|timeout> layer L want W reached R (atomic A, copy signal S, signal copy|host), queued Q (front layer F), X ms` when a wait starts, ends or times out (the first 400 waits and every timeout); `queued` is the depth of the host queue of `idle_pace` and F the layer of its first step. What to look for in the next failing run: `timeout ... - the queue still holds a layer the wait needs (not issued)` means `pace(layer, 1)` left a step of a layer <= L in the queue (an issue-order or `idleq.failed` defect in the host queue); `queued 0` with `reached` stuck means every copy was issued and the signal never rose (the stream or the landing signal: then `signal copy` against `host` and the stream order decide). Run it in spec 88's whole arm sequence, not alone (114a-d did not reproduce).
  RESULT 2026-11-16 (T-646, T-647, T-648): all three arms RAN, no `never landed` and no GATE-TIMEOUT breadcrumb, including 114c with the default signal and the default 4096 KiB a phase: the crash is not reproduced by a single-arm run (n=2). So the signal, the late issue and the queue order are not each sufficient causes; spec 88 failed at its sixth arm in one server sequence, so look at what that sequence did before (the five earlier arms' state; spec 95 logged the abtest warning `only 17-19 GiB of free RAM in 2 MiB blocks` against the 33 GiB arena on every arm, spec 88 was not checked). The bases of 114b and 114c ran slow (ORDER EFFECT, -23..-29% base-late), so no speed verdict.
- OPEN 2026-11-16 (T-602): `STRATA_MMVQ_IL_PLAIN=1` (opt-in) crashes at layer 0 (`an illegal memory access`). Code read
  (unconfirmed static analysis): on gfx1100 the plain-column rows table is all 0, so the projections fall back to native_mmvq;
  what changes is the HC read: with `q8_il` set, fused_gr.cu skips the RDNA read (`STRATA_GR_RDNA=1` in fast-hip) and the 32-column
  up kernels for the generic read + `gr_q8_tail`, which writes the interleaved copy. Chain 13 also runs 114f (`STRATA_GR_RDNA=0`
  alone), 114e (IL_PLAIN with `GR_RDNA=0`) and 114d (IL_PLAIN with `STRATA_MMVQ_IL_CHECK=1`, `AMD_SERIALIZE_KERNEL=3`,
  `HIP_LAUNCH_BLOCKING=1`: names the faulting kernel). The box agent's `mmvq_il_parity` run (section 5 item 2) stays as planned.
  RESULT 2026-11-16 (T-649, T-650, and 114d T-678: IL_PLAIN with the check env, serialized launches and `GR_RDNA=1` also ran with no fault, text identical, speed equal to base; no IL line in its logs, so inertness is not excluded): 114f (`GR_RDNA=0` alone) and 114e (IL_PLAIN with `GR_RDNA=0`) both RAN with no fault, both -11..-13% against the early base (the same cost: IL_PLAIN adds about 0); so the generic HC read is not the fault, and the crash needs the RDNA read with `q8_il` (`GR_RDNA=1`). 114d did not name any kernel: nothing faulted.
  CODE FOUND 2026-11-16 (T-713; by reading, NOT confirmed on the box, engine not built): a concrete way to the fault. The fast-hip preset sets `STRATA_GR_RDNA=1` and `STRATA_GR_RDNA_Q8=1`, and with both `generate.cpp` (`skip.insert` of `hc_attn_down/up`, `hc_ffn_down/up`) never loads the BF16 rows of those four projections: `wd/wu->data` is null, only `hc_r8` exists. With `STRATA_MMVQ_IL_PLAIN=1` (il build) `verify.cpp` sets `q8_il`, `fused_gr_read_multi` then skips the RDNA read (which is the only reader of the int8 copies) and runs the generic read that writes the copy, whose kernels read `w_down` / `w_up`: a null pointer at layer 0 = `an illegal memory access`. It also fits every observation: `GR_RDNA=0` loads the BF16 rows (114e fine), and 114d most likely ran a build without `-DSTRATA_HIP_MMVQ_IL` (IL_PLAIN is then off, no IL line in its log). GUARD (in `fused_gr_read_multi_slice`, rule `diag::il_copy_generic_read_ok`): when a BF16 set is absent and its int8 copy present, `q8_il` is cleared, ONE line is printed (`strata: STRATA_MMVQ_IL_PLAIN: the hyper-connection down/up weights exist only as int8 copies ... T-602`), the RDNA read runs and the projections read the plain columns; output is the same, nothing changes where `q8_il` is null (every default run). No `STRATA_IL_DEBUG` was added: the defect is concrete. What to look for: spec 85 `il-plain` on the `build-win-il` build: the line above in the engine log, no crash, text equal to `il-build`; if it still crashes at layer 0, run it with `STRATA_LAUNCH_CHECK_VERBOSE=1` `AMD_SERIALIZE_KERNEL=3` and read the kernel name; and a build without the define must print nothing (IL_PLAIN is inert there).

  now supplies VRAM, temperature and power; the name comes from the PCI id when product_name is empty (the 7900 XT's is) and the
  label is HIP when the readings come from the sysfs reader (`backend_label`, display only).

## 10. Architecture facts worth knowing before touching the code

- Chunk plan: the serve loop cuts at root_at (first `<|im_start|>` after token 0 if >= `--prompt-cache-root` 2048; a
  smaller system prompt is re-read on purpose, ~0.3 s), turn_at (last `<|im_start|>`) and n-1; `CACHE_MESSAGE` /
  `TOOL_BOUNDARY` and `pin=` add cuts; <= `--short-read` 64 tokens go through decode windows; chunk = min(prefill_chunk,
  segment rounded up to 256) via lend -> planned_chunk -> equal_chunk -> request_chunk (generate.cpp ~997, ~1009,
  ~10355; loop in `Prefill::run_impl`; `STRATA_TRACE` prints `prompt chunk` / `lent`). The loan lasts until the segment
  ends.
- Auto chunk: largest 256-multiple with loan <= 90% of slots (85% if less pinned), 128 left; AUTO_MAX clamps
  32768/16384/8192; loan at 32K = 9,413/10,733 slots, 12.59/14.37 GiB, ~5% resident (generate.cpp ~3938).
- With CPU share armed, `stream_all_min` = `CPU_SHARE_MAX` 4096: T >= 4096 streams (192-slot ring, copies overlap
  attention); T < 4096 is staged (per-layer sync on routing, 8-slot ring, 32 with MMQ groups). A warm turn is one staged
  chunk and the copy engine idles through attention (the reason for PF_AHEAD). CPU share only takes experts with <= 8
  tokens (MAXT, expert.hpp:143); share=auto keeps running means (w 0.25), clamp(g/(c+g), 0.05, 0.9), needs 3 ratios,
  probes every 29th layer, HIP off except a single gfx12.
- EQUAL_TAIL (prefill_plan.hpp:182, `_MIN` 256) is skipped when evened chunks would be < 8,192 (kEqualTailMinChunk, :172;
  upstream measured 25 tokens past 8,192 read 25% slower when evened) or below the staged limit; `PREFILL_EQUAL` (#693)
  evens only when the last chunk would stream anyway; EQUAL_TAIL wins on a staged tail.
- Checkpoints: `--prompt-cache 6` (generate.cpp ~961), ~113-118 MiB each, at the turn boundary and chunk ends >= 16,384
  apart; restore only from a prefix (GDN state). Parked conversations: 1,280-6,656 MiB auto (usually 4,352), 4 slots;
  snapshot = 111 MiB + 14.9 KiB/token + 113 MiB per checkpoint. Refill is one wait (~1 s for a 12.6 GiB loan;
  `STRATA_REFILL_BLOCKING` keeps the old form; trace prints `refilled ... in <ms>`).
- Prefix reuse breaks on: client edits; system prompt or tool list change (tools render in the system block -> full
  re-read); effort; a re-render that differs; dropped reasoning (re-renders only the last turn); eviction; loop-guard
  collapse.
- Floor model: 48 layers x 512 experts, top-10, 1.4 MB/expert, 44% resident, routed share 1-exp(-10n/512), floor ~0.67 s,
  PCIe-bound. QSA indexer cost grows n^2, attention ~2,051 cells/query; KV staging ~1,056 B/token/layer over 12 QSA layers.
- Hot path hygiene: no per-window getenv (c44b1ad7b, 7c0f03228), no hot-path allocations, no decode file I/O; the 4
  dispatch loops in `expert_pool_dispatch_multi` are linear (`first_of`, `miss`, `next_of`, `q_of`; greedy check owed).
  Default-on: `ADAPT_PERSIST`, `UNBUFFERED_LOAD`, `HIPBLASLT_DESC_CACHE`, `PIN_PARK_TOKENS`, `PSI_GUARD`. `ARENA_WARM`
  removed; `combine_multi_hits` kept (removal = fork drift).
- Status page: the field `spec = 12` is the effective window cap; the preset runs `--spec 4` (draft depth) with
  `--lookup-max-t 12`.
- llama.cpp v1.0.0 (tag 2026-11-13, 249 commits after v0.9.0) candidates, read against our code: V1 `e95426f16` covered by
  `--pcie-frac` / `pcie_share_for` / `calibrate_ratio.hpp`; V2 `cf34d3113` covered by `ROUTE_PUBLISH=1`; V3 ClassLRU
  `c4fb57969` dropped (T-452); V4 `69ee2ecc0` layout already in `Tables.tiles` / `moe_fused_iq.cu`, only the int8 path's
  fixed tile width differed (built and dropped, T-107); V5 dropped (T-560); V6 dropped (T-569); U1, U3 dropped (T-571,
  T-570); U4, U5 covered; U6 not applicable (ggml internals).
- Code tools: graphify (`graphify update .`, `graphify-out/` git-excluded), ast-grep 0.51 + MCP, tree-sitter MCP needs
  `mcp<2`.
- Platform: ROCm 10 has no `hipMemcpyBatchAsync`, so `STRATA_DMA_BATCH` is CUDA-only. The Flash-Next "IQ2_XS" file holds
  IQ2_S / IQ2_XXS / IQ1_M gate/up with Q2_0 down (~176.9B parameters); its head is IQ4_XS. Q2_0 and IQ2_XS share one
  n-gram table file (same SHA-256). English 40.5K draft vocab is best for English/code (+5%, ~215 MiB less VRAM); zh60k
  helps Chinese only. KV quant: q8_0 costs < 0.00012 KL; 4-bit adds 0.0016-0.0027 KL and grows 15-30% from 4K to 32K;
  keys are ~2x as sensitive as values.
- strata-poly (Qwen3.6-35B): one CPU thread per physical core (8; SMT adds no bandwidth; poly does not pin threads);
  with the expert cache on it is not run-to-run deterministic (swaps and CPU/GPU rounding part texts after 9-81 tokens),
  so exactness checks need `--no-gpu-experts --expert-swaps 0`; Q8 KV 11.7 KB/token vs strata fp32 45 KB/token; prompt
  reading is GPU-compute bound (attention 38% of a 42K read), decode CPU experts ~half and RAM-bound (33.4 GB/s).
  Reproduce on CUDA: build `strata strata-poly poly_gdn_cols_test poly_gemm_small_test` with
  `-DCMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES=86-real`; daily poly flags `--max-context 131072 --threads 8 --kv-q8 --attn-split
  --graph-experts --elastic-prefill 1024 --lend-staging --stage-next --suffix-drafts 256 --cpu-multi`.
- gpu-unlock (Zagitta, GPL-2.0, RTX 30 only) lifts FP16/BF16/TF32 MMA with FP32 accumulate (48.3 vs ~25 TFLOPS),
  bit-identical; it helps prompt attention, not INT8 MMQ, CPU experts or decode (T-545). Install: `make install`,
  `gpu_unlock.targets=0000:10:00.0` on the kernel command line, check `journalctl -k -b | grep gpu_unlock`.
- Bench cases: read1k..read16k (16 new tokens), short (34-token chat, 256 new), edit (5.3K code rewrite, 512 new), long
  (41.8K prompt, 128 new); "cold" = first run in a fresh engine (later runs hit the conversation cache).
- The CPU-compute vs PCIe-promote cost per expert varies ~10x across platforms (42 vs 115 us here), so calibration must
  use window time, not a static table (PcieAuto picked 0.20-0.25 and lost). `HSA_ENABLE_SDMA=0` tips conflict with the
  measured SDMA gains here.
- Agent replay (`tools/pancake/replay_turns.py SPEC --out DIR [--dry-run|--arms a,b|--synthetic|--reps 2|--max-gap 2]`):
  rebuilds a session from `pancake-turns.jsonl` sizes. A request >= 8,192 tokens extends the conversation it can grow
  from (last prompt <= it <= last + output + 16K). A `reused == 0` re-read stays in that conversation. Smaller requests
  are side requests. Turn N+1 = turn N + the real reply + a `<tool_response>` sized to within 2 tokens (server tokenizer
  and template). Output: per-request park/restore/evict lines; per arm, cold re-reads (non-first turn > 8K, reuse < 50%),
  prompt time, peak RSS, min MemAvailable. Spec 11 `replay` block: 2026-11-09 16:13-16:43, base vs cache2048-min8192;
  add `base1280` when today's auto budget differs.
- Window trace: `STRATA_WINDOW_TRACE=/tmp/w.stwt ./run-pancake.sh iq2_xs fast`, one greedy request, then
  `tools/pancake/trace/wtrace.py` and `tail_replay.py --us-per-miss 23` (edit: 60). `_EVERY=N`, `_MAX_MB` (2048).
  Per row: drafter probability (NaN for lookup) and per-layer placement. No top-1 margin or entropy (the stale
  `tools/pancake/trace/corpus-recorder-engine.patch` would add gate weights and norms; it no longer applies).
- Kernel ports must be re-measured per GPU (launch ~14-16 us gfx1100 vs ~3 us sm_86; DPP vs warp intrinsics; WMMA vs mma).
  Server, drafting, routing and CPU-kernel items transfer; re-check only the size of the gain.
- BFLA (`src/kernels/cuda/bfla.cu`, `include/strata/kernels/bfla.hpp`, q36_engine.cu; arXiv 2605.12193, Apache-2.0
  reference read, not copied). Engine: qwen35 only (10 dense gated attention layers, H 16, HKV 2, D 256). It replaces the
  path that materialises FP32 scores over the whole prefix (FP16 K/V, hipBLAS QK^T, causal softmax to FP16, PV GEMM),
  which is quadratic in FLOPs and score traffic (~12 B/score). Not on Flash-Next: QSA already reads 2,051 cells/query;
  a V/3 budget reads ~16x more at 128K (tile-shared selection: T-098, dropped).
  Kernels (HIP wave32 gfx11; other builds return false):
  `features`: phi = N(mean over G heads of N(q_h)), adjacent pairs summed to 128, N again; mu = block mean over K.
  FP32, no FP8. Hadamard dropped (relu(phi.mu) is rotation-invariant). mu recomputed over the prefix every chunk
  (~1/4 of `k_kv_f16`), so a restored cache is never stale.
  `select`: one workgroup per (128/G-token tile, KV head); online LSE of relu(phi.mu)/sqrt(128); budget
  k = ceil(min(V, max(Kmin, min(Kmax, ceil(eta V))))/64). Mandatory 2 sink + own + 4 local blocks count in the quota.
  32-step bisection for the k-th score, ties to the smaller id, deterministic ascending list.
  `attn`: 4 waves per (64 rows, KV head), 16 rows x 256 dims per wave; Q in LDS once; 16-key steps, V transposed in LDS;
  16 WMMAs each for S and PV (O = 128 VGPRs/lane); causal mask only on diagonal blocks; exp2 online softmax in FP32;
  P to FP16 via a per-wave LDS tile; FP32 out, gate stays `k_gate_mul`. Not bitwise vs the GEMM path.
  gfx11 facts: `v_wmma_f32_16x16x16_f16` A lane l = row l%16 (16-31 repeat), B = column l%16, D lane l = D[2i+l/16][l%16];
  64 KiB LDS/workgroup; no FP8 matrix ops, no TMA/wgmma; every wave does the same work.
  Follows the paper's Ratio-K, not the published code (keep-mass 0.99, 256-blocks, 8 local).
  Switches: `STRATA_BFLA_DENSE=1` (exact), `_SPARSE=1` (lossy, wins if both), `_MIN` 32768, `_ETA` 1/3, `_KMIN` 2048,
  `_KMAX` 32768, `_SINK` 2, `_LOCAL` 4, `_TAIL` 1024/G; plain `STRATA_BFLA` is ignored.
  Estimate (not measured): 81,920 N^2 FLOP; ~45 s at 128K (30-45% of prefill), ~180 s at 256K; Ratio-K keeps
  1/1.4 (32K), 1/1.8 (64K), 1/3.2 (128K), 1/4.9 (256K).
  Gate: `bfla_test`, `bfla_cpu_test`, needles at 64K/128K + teacher-forced top-1 vs dense, then TTFT A/B (T-299 KL fails 1e-3).
- Windows CUDA sm_75 (GTX 1660 Ti 6 GB, CUDA 12.8.61, VS 2022 Build Tools MSVC 14.44, CMake 4.4.4, Ninja 1.13.2):
  run `vcvars64.bat` from the VS Build Tools (`VC\Auxiliary\Build`); `cmake -S . -B build-sm75 -G Ninja
  -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release -DSTRATA_ENABLE_CUDA=ON -DCMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES=75 -DCMAKE_CUDA_RUNTIME_LIBRARY=Shared`;
  `cmake --build build-sm75 -j 4 --target strata strata-q36 strata-poly`. Without Shared, strata-q36 fails with LNK2005
  (setup.py gets Shared via `STRATA_EXPERIMENTAL_SM60=ON`, CMakeLists.txt ~222). One portability fix was needed:
  `std::popcount` instead of a GCC builtin in `kmiss_tier.hpp`.
  Test compile errors: `q5k_head_test` (`std::to_string`, no `<string>`), `flag_order_test` (`__atomic_load_n`),
  `gemm_w16_parity` (`setenv`), `poly_kv_q4_test` (kv_q4_test.cu:342 range-for). Decode with `tools/pancake/bench_win.py
  RUNS WARMUP MEASURED NEW` (gates only system CPU < 12% before each run). The first requests are ~10x slower: warm up.
  Untested: long context, app, vision, CUDA 13, test_phase, quality gate. Results: T-550..T-552.
  **STATUS 2026-11-17 (T-794, T-795):** the latest commit (78d504fdd) builds `strata-poly` for sm_75 with MSVC 14.44 and CUDA 12.8 with 0 errors (about 75 min at -j 4, llama.cpp fetched). Decode against the 2026-11-10 binary, paired in time (old, new, old, new; 3 x 3 measured each): about 29 tok/s both, NO CHANGE inside the 2-4 tok/s noise (other processes held system CPU at 30-50%); T-550's 34 tok/s was not reproduced by either binary in this session. This is strata-poly on a Qwen3.6 file, not the daily Flash-Next engine. The new switches cannot act on this card: STRATA_FATTN and STRATA_FDEC need an Ampere GPU, the only other STRATA_* names src/poly reads (SWAP_HALF, SOFTMAX_REG, GEMM_SMALL_TILED, GDN_COLS, ROUTE_TRACE, ROUTE_TAIL_SKIP, CPU_STATS) were not tried here.
  **TESTS 2026-11-17 (T-796..T-798):** `strata_tests` builds for sm_75 with MSVC (5 test-source portability fixes, no engine file touched). `ctest -L cpu`: 180 of 188 pass, 8 skipped (model file / ISA). `ctest -L gpu`: 174 pass, 12 skipped (sm_80+ kernels, HIP-only), 2 open sm_75 limits: `pf_wmma_parity` (WK=256 tile does not launch at 1280 x 2560, probable 64 KiB shared-memory limit) and `cuda_spill_guard` (474 sm_75 kernels use stack, allow-list is for the daily arch). One new-code defect fixed: `head_argmax_parity` ran 1..12 columns against an 8-column verify window. `gemm_w16_parity` needs a 250 s limit on this card (default 180 s times out).
  **LOCAL BENCH AND INT8 CHECK 2026-11-17 (T-867, T-868):** strata-poly on the current build, short 30.22 +-5.03 / code 32.34 +-1.70 tok/s (3 x 3 x 96 tokens): NO change against T-550 / T-794 / T-786, inside the PC's noise. The promoted `STRATA_*` flags exist only in the full `strata` engine (33 GB arena, cannot run on this card); the poly flags were all tried (T-786, T-787). Owner's int8 question: strata-poly already runs llama.cpp's int8 MMQ for the prompt experts and links the `__dp4a` kernels of `strata_kernels`; the `src/split` dp4a files and `FNX_PFX_ATTN_I8` are strata-split (multi-GPU, not poly) and the int8 attention is sm_80+ only. Nothing to port (static reading, T-868).
  **LOCAL TESTS, EVENING 2026-11-17 (T-869..T-873):** tools/pancake 777 passed / 0 failed / 78 skipped; `ctest -L cpu` 178 passed, 8 skipped, ple_reader_selftest FAILS on wall-clock checks (keep-alive read count at a 20 ms period, injected-delay check) on this loaded PC and needs a quiet run (open, not an engine defect); `ctest -L gpu` 173 passed, 12 skipped, pf_wmma_parity and cuda_spill_guard as before, gemm_w16_parity UNDECIDED (crash 0xc0000409 at 47 s, then a 915 s timeout, both while another agent's GPU tests shared the card; rerun it alone); 26 host rule tests clean under ASan+UBSan and the three fuzz harnesses clean. No new defect found.
  **F16 PROMPT PATH FOR strata-poly, 2026-11-17 (T-842..T-844, owner's own PC, noisy): NOT PORTED, NO VALUE.** Question: does the 7900 XT's STRATA_PF_F16 idea (expert prompt matmuls on f16 matrix cores) give strata-poly a gain on the 1660 Ti? The poly prompt MoE (engine.cpp `moe_rows`) already runs every expert through llama.cpp's int8 MMQ; current prompt speed (`tools/pancake/bench_win_prompt.py`, new) 44 / 101 / 132 tok/s at 500 / 1000 / 1800 tokens (sd 6 / 22 / 11; 500 tokens take about as long as 1800: a per-batch PCIe staging and CPU cost dominates); nsys: routed experts are about 54% of the GPU kernel time (IQ2_XXS 36%, IQ3_XXS 17%), dense Q5_K/Q6_K 28%, host-to-device staging copies 9.2 s per 2 requests. Premise fails on the hardware: TU116 (GTX 16 series) has no tensor cores and the f16 `wmma` path measures 1.59 TFLOPS against 6.9 TOPS for the int8 `wmma` path MMQ already uses (4.4x slower), so an f16 expert kernel cannot beat it; the best case (an infinitely fast expert kernel) would save about 40% of the wall. On a card with real f16 matrix cores and more VRAM (fewer staged experts) the question reopens; here it stays closed. Unconfirmed static analysis + profile, no f16 kernel was written, no switch added.
  **STRATA-POLY PROMPT STAGING, 2026-11-17 (T-849, T-850, T-899, T-900): `stage_ahead` DEFAULT CHANGED 1024 -> 128 (engine.hpp, `--stage-ahead 1024` restores the old).** Calls of fewer than 1024 tokens (every 500-1000 token prompt) used to route first and copy only the routed experts with nothing copied ahead; poly already had the copy-ahead (second stream, pinned double buffers), the default just kept it off below 1024. Decision rule (owner): keep 128 only if logits parity passes and the gain is clear at 500 / 1000 with no loss at 1800: parity PASSES bit for bit (poly_stage_ahead_parity_test, one engine, 500 / 1000 / 1800 tokens, T-899), steady prompt speed 500 tokens 77 -> 144 tok/s, 1000 tokens 105 -> 206, 1800 tokens 184 -> 196 (no loss), every 128 rep above every 1024 rep at 500 / 1000 (T-900, owner's PC at 55-100% CPU). `--stage-next` adds nothing (T-849). Open: two loads of the same model in one process can give different logits (up to 0.5) with the same setting, which is why greedy ids differ between runs (not stage_ahead; cause not found, suspect the load-time expert cache); the first request of each new prompt size is 2-4x slower in both arms (cold 500 / 1000 requests not better with 128); thresholds below 128 and `--stage-mib` untested; the 7900 XT / 3060 presets were tuned separately (T-092) and a preset that sets `--stage-ahead` is unaffected.

## 11. Where things are

- Plan: this file. Tests: TEST_LEDGER.md. Leads: LEADS.md. Agent and build rules: AGENTS.md. Measured results for
  readers: README section 2; switch census: README section 6; credits: README section 9; quants: `docs/QUANTS.md`.
- Raw evidence (kept): `bench/specs/`, `bench/results/`, `pancake-docs/research/bench/` (results archives, run scripts,
  night-queue logs), `bench/prefetch/`, `bench/quality/`, `data/fork-kb/` (fork knowledge base; refresh with
  `GITHUB_TOKEN=... python3 tools/forks/fork_kb.py`, works on Windows too; last scan 0004-20261112).
- Documentation rule: only MOD_PLAN.md, TEST_LEDGER.md and LEADS.md are maintained (plus AGENTS.md and README.md);
  no other report, note, status or findings file may be created (AGENTS.md "Documentation rule").
- Bring-up after a reboot (Linux box):
  1. `ls /home/dan/clone-of-d/src/Strata`; if it does not mount: `sudo ntfsfix -d /dev/nvme0n1p5` (ntfs3 writeback hang
     2026-11-10, engine ~25% slower afterwards; T-287, T-290). After the 2026-11-13 reboots the volume came back dirty and
     was force-mounted: `sudo mount -t ntfs3 -o force,uid=1000,gid=1000,umask=022
     /dev/disk/by-uuid/401E033B1E03298C /home/dan/clone-of-d`.
  1b. The GPU clock resets to `auto` on reboot; pin it before measuring:
     `echo high | sudo tee /sys/class/drm/card1/device/power_dpm_force_performance_level`. Commands shown with a `!`
     prefix are for the agent session; in a normal terminal drop the `!`.
  2. `git fsck --no-dangling && git status -sb`; if broken, clone from GitHub or `git fetch` the all-refs bundle.
  3. `sudo tools/pancake/os_tune.sh cpu-install`, then `tools/pancake/os_tune.sh` (large free 2 MiB blocks).
  4. `cmake --build build-hip -j16 --target strata` (not `all`: strata-poly is CUDA-only).
  5. `./run-pancake.sh` (8080; `http://localhost:8080/v1`, model `qwen3.8-flash-next-iq2_xs`); check `/`, `/status` or
     `/pancake`, and `serve/web/monitor.html`.
  6. Before measuring: restore checks + `ctest -L gpu` (baseline 122/125, T-346; latest 133/134, T-367).
